# WhatsApp call-log analysis — for the app development team

Source: ERPNext **Call Log**, `custom_source = WhatsApp`. Window: 04 Oct – 10 Oct 2026. Generated 10 Oct 2026 from production. Read-only extract; nothing was changed.

> **Privacy:** this file contains chat/group titles and phone numbers from employees' phones. Share it only inside the team.

## 1. Key numbers

| Metric | Value |
|---|---|
| Total WhatsApp rows | **1315** (14.5% of all 9060 Call Logs) |
| Rows that are NOT calls (message / system notifications) | **1217** (92.5%) |
| Voice / video call events | **98** (7.5%) |
| Call events with duration > 0 (real, completed calls) | **21** (1.6%) |
| Rows with direction = Unknown | 1271 |
| Devices sending WhatsApp rows / devices sending any calls | 13 / 33 |
| Unknown rows that carry a phone number (`from`/`to`) | 119 of 1271 (9.4%) |
| Unknown rows linked to a Lead / Customer | 22 of 1271 (1.7%) |
| Exact twin rows (same user + title + start time) | 160 rows in **80 pairs** (≈80 extra rows, 6.1%) |
| Record names containing a newline character | 598 (45.5%) |
| Record names not matching the usual pattern | 3 (see section 7) |
| `end_time` filled / WhatsApp app version filled | 21 / 0 of 1315 |
| Package | Business: 1218 · Personal: 97 · other/unknown: 0 |

|---|---:|---:|---|
| System/empty | 478 | 37.6% | WhatsApp Business |
| Person/chat | 367 | 28.9% | Mordernity Photo Kerala |
| Group message | 247 | 19.4% | South Zone Reporting Group: Swati Splashjet |
| Group batch | 143 | 11.3% | Courier Details  (25 messages): Saksham Sir |
| Number title | 36 | 2.8% | +27 64 686 8929 |

## 2. Findings

1. **The app records WhatsApp notifications as call rows.** About 92% of rows are chat, group or system notifications, not calls.
1. **Every device that sends WhatsApp rows sends both kinds** (message notifications and call events). Volume follows how busy the user's WhatsApp is, so the behaviour is in the app's capture logic, not in one phone.
1. **Same event sent twice.** Many notifications arrive twice ~5 ms apart, once with a hash in the id and once with `null` (section 8).
1. **The Call Log id is a composite** `WA-device-0|package|code|hash\n|notificationId:epoch-ms` — it contains pipes and, for the hash variant, a trailing newline (section 7).
1. **`contact name` is the raw notification title**: group names, `Group: Sender`, `Group (25 messages): Sender`, or just `WhatsApp Business`. Non-breaking spaces make one title appear in variants.
1. **Numbers are rarely present**, so rows cannot be matched to Leads/Customers; direction is Unknown for almost every row.
1. **Floods:** up to 150 rows in one hour from a single device (section 5).
1. **Fields we never receive:** WhatsApp app version; end time (except 21 call rows); the number behind a saved contact name.
1. **Capture starts and stops per device** (see per-day table) — consistent with notification access being granted/removed or an app update; the server does not record app versions, so this cannot be confirmed from data.

## 3. Suggested changes for the app (for discussion)

- Send **only call notifications** (voice/video: `Notification.category = call` or the call-style title), not message notifications. Biggest single win.
- Send **one event per notification**: use the notification key/id + timestamp and drop the `null`-hash twin.
- Send fields **separately instead of packing them in the id**: package, notification category/type, notification id, timestamp, title, (text only if privacy allows).
- Send the **phone number** when the title is a number; send saved-contact name **and** number when available.
- Send a real **direction** (incoming / outgoing / missed), **duration** and **end_time** for calls.
- Send **app version** (this app and WhatsApp) and notification-access status; add a **per-device rate limit** for bursts.
- **Normalise titles on the phone** (trim, replace non-breaking spaces; split `Group: Sender` and `(N messages)`).

## 7. Call Log id format

```
WA-<deviceId>-<n>|<package>|<code>|<hash or null>|<notificationId>:<epoch-ms>
```
- `deviceId`: 16-hex Android ID. `package`: `com.whatsapp.w4b` (Business) or `com.whatsapp` (personal).
- `code`: `1` and `11` appear only on rows without a direction (message-like); `23` appears on every row with a real direction (**inferred, not confirmed — the app code is not on the server**).
- `hash`: 92 distinct non-null values; the hash ends with a newline character inside the id (598 ids contain one).
- **3 ids use a different shape** (`code = 2147483647`, token `ranker_group`; looks like Android's system notification group key and a default placeholder — inferred):

  - `WA-b9755a6a8bc48c60-0\|com.whatsapp.w4b\|2147483647\|ranker_group\|10339\|ranker_group:1791451360783` — Abhishek Pillaey, no title
  - `WA-b9755a6a8bc48c60-0\|com.whatsapp.w4b\|2147483647\|ranker_group\|10339\|ranker_group:1791452151660` — Abhishek Pillaey, no title
  - `WA-b9755a6a8bc48c60-0\|com.whatsapp.w4b\|2147483647\|ranker_group\|10339\|ranker_group:1791453350206` — Abhishek Pillaey, no title

## 11. Notes and limits

- “Notification vs call” is judged from the summary text the app sends (starts with `WhatsApp Voice` / `WhatsApp Video`).
- “Device” is the Android ID the app sends; it identifies the phone, not the account.
- Times are the phone's start time as reported; “created” on the server can be minutes to hours later (sync delay).
- Counts come from the Call Log table as of the extract time; new rows arrive continuously.
